## Account Transfers & Standing Instructions

Total: **26** (positive: 7, negative: 12, edge: 7)

### Positive Tests

| TC ID | WF Ref | Test Case | Preconditions | Steps | Expected Result | Priority |
|-------|--------|-----------|---------------|-------|-----------------|----------|
| TC-001 | WF-001 | Submit Account Transfer with sufficient available balance | User logged in as <role>, Source account has available balance >= <valid transfer amount> | 1. Navigate to the Account Transfers page<br>2. Select <From Office> in the From Office field<br>3. Select <From Client> in the From Client field<br>4. Select <From Account Type> from the From Account Type dropdown<br>5. Select <From Account> in the From Account field<br>6. Select <To Office> in the To Office field<br>7. Select <To Client> in the To Client field<br>8. Select <To Account Type> from the To Account Type dropdown<br>9. Select <To Account> in the To Account field<br>10. Enter <valid transfer amount> in the Transfer Amount field (<= available balance)<br>11. Enter <valid transfer date> in the Transfer Date field<br>12. Enter <optional description> in the Description field<br>13. Click Submit | processes transfer: debits the source account and credits the destination account | high |
| TC-002 | WF-005 | Create Standing Instruction with Recurrence Type = Periodic (recurrence fields visible) | User logged in as <role>, Standing Instructions page is accessible | 1. Navigate to the Standing Instructions page<br>2. Click + Create Standing Instruction<br>3. Enter <Name> in the Name field<br>4. Select <From Account> in the From Account field<br>5. Select <To Account> in the To Account field<br>6. Select <Instruction Type> from the Instruction Type dropdown<br>7. Enter <Amount> in the Amount field (if Instruction Type requires an amount)<br>8. Enter <Validity From date> in the Validity From field<br>9. Enter <Validity Till date> in the Validity Till field<br>10. Select 'Periodic' in the Recurrence Type dropdown<br>11. After the Login page is shown, attempt to navigate to an authenticated-only URL within the application (e.g., enter/paste the authenticated page URL in the browser address bar and press Enter) | Attempting to open the authenticated page redirects to the Login page and the protected content is not displayed | medium |
